# Workout of the Day library (365 workouts, solo + team of 2)

Durations are the main workout only. Add a 10 min warm-up and 5 min cool-down.

Numbers written a/b are men/women. Race weights (Open): sled push 152/102 kg, sled pull 103/78 kg, farmers 2x24/2x16 kg, sandbag 20/10 kg, wall ball 6/4 kg.

Every workout has a **Solo** and a **Team of 2** version. The team version adds work and says how to share it.


## Week 1

### Day 1 · Monday · Blackout Pull
*Strength endurance · 44 min · moderate*

**Solo**
```
EMOM 44

Minute 1-4 rotation, 11 rounds:
- 8/6 cal SkiErg
- 14 goblet lunges (22.5/15 kg)
- 10 m sandbag walking lunges (20/10 kg)
- 8 burpee dual-DB step-overs (2x22.5/15 kg)
```
**Team of 2**
```
Team of 2: EMOM 44
You go, I go: inside each minute, P1 starts and P2 finishes the reps (split as you wish). Any run is done together.

Minute 1-4 rotation, 11 rounds:
- 10/8 cal SkiErg
- 18 goblet lunges (22.5/15 kg)
- 15 m sandbag walking lunges (20/10 kg)
- 10 burpee dual-DB step-overs (2x22.5/15 kg)
```
Score: Rounds completed inside the minute

### Day 2 · Tuesday · Hollow Climb
*Compromised running · 43 min · hard*

**Solo**
```
9 sets for max distance: 3 min on / 2 min off

Cycle through the list in order until all 9 sets are done; rest 2 min between sets:
- 3 min max run
- 3 min max BikeErg
- 3 min max farmers carry (2x24/2x16 kg)
- 3 min max sled push (152/102 kg)
```
**Team of 2**
```
Team of 2: 9 sets for max distance: 3 min on / 2 min off
You go, I go: one partner works while the other rests, switch every 30 s, so you can go harder. On the run, both go together. Score the team total.

Cycle through the list in order until all 9 sets are done; rest 2 min between sets:
- 3 min max run
- 3 min max BikeErg
- 3 min max farmers carry (2x24/2x16 kg)
- 3 min max sled push (152/102 kg)
```
Score: Total metres

### Day 3 · Wednesday · Copper Storm
*Team of 2 · 46 min · moderate*

**Team of 2**
```
Team of 2: For time, time cap 46 min
Run every leg together. Split each station as you wish; the order of stations does not matter.

- 400 m run synchro (run together)
- 37.5 m sled push (152/102 kg)
- 400 m run synchro (run together)
- 650 m SkiErg
- 400 m run synchro (run together)
- 50 KB swings (24/16 kg)
- 400 m run synchro (run together)
- 650 m row
- 400 m run synchro (run together)
- 65 m sandbag walking lunges (20/10 kg)
- 400 m run synchro (run together)
- 65 m DB farmers lunges (2x22.5/15 kg)
- 400 m run synchro (run together)
- 65 wall balls (6/4 kg)
- 400 m run synchro (run together)
- 37.5 m sled pull (103/78 kg)
- 400 m run synchro
```
**Solo**
```
For time, time cap 46 min

- 400 m run
- 25 m sled push (152/102 kg)
- 400 m run
- 500 m SkiErg
- 400 m run
- 40 KB swings (24/16 kg)
- 400 m run
- 500 m row
- 400 m run
- 50 m sandbag walking lunges (20/10 kg)
- 400 m run
- 50 m DB farmers lunges (2x22.5/15 kg)
- 400 m run
- 50 wall balls (6/4 kg)
- 400 m run
- 25 m sled pull (103/78 kg)
- 400 m run
```
Score: Time

### Day 4 · Thursday · Copper Bridge
*Engine · 48 min · moderate*

**Solo**
```
Every 4 min for 48 min (12 rounds)

- min 1-3: max cal, rotate assault/echo bike > row > SkiErg
- min 4: 8 burpee broad jumps
- @ max sustainable pace for all
```
**Team of 2**
```
Team of 2: Every 4 min for 48 min (12 rounds)
You go, I go: P1 takes the machine while P2 does the station reps, then swap roles each round. The station reps are doubled because you get the whole interval for them.

- min 1-3: max cal, rotate assault/echo bike > row > SkiErg
- min 4: 16 burpee broad jumps
- @ max sustainable pace for all
```
Score: Total calories

### Day 5 · Friday · Storm Rally
*Race simulation · 40 min · hard*

**Solo**
```
7 rounds for time, time cap 40 min

7 rounds:
- 18/14 cal assault/echo bike
- 400 m run
- 16 goblet lunges (22.5/15 kg)
rest 1 min between rounds
```
**Team of 2**
```
Team of 2: 7 rounds for time, time cap 40 min
Run together, side by side. Split the station work however you like, one partner working at a time, and swap often.

7 rounds:
- 25/19 cal assault/echo bike
- 400 m run
- 20 goblet lunges (22.5/15 kg)
rest 1 min between rounds
```
Score: Time

### Day 6 · Saturday · Ottawa Climb
*Light aerobic · 42 min · easy*

**Solo**
```
28 min easy run + EMOM 14 technique

Part A:
- 28 min easy run (zone 2)

Part B (EMOM 14):
- odd: 50 m farmers carry (2x24/2x16 kg)
- even: 12.5 m sled pull (103/78 kg)
- light load, perfect race technique
```
**Team of 2**
```
Team of 2: 28 min easy run + EMOM 14 technique
Do it side by side at a pace where you can both talk the whole time.

Part A:
- 28 min easy run (zone 2)

Part B (EMOM 14):
P1 starts on the odd station and P2 on the even one; swap every minute and coach each other.
- odd: 50 m farmers carry (2x24/2x16 kg)
- even: 12.5 m sled pull (103/78 kg)
- light load, perfect race technique
```
Score: None, quality only

### Day 7 · Sunday · Northern Shift
*Active recovery (optional) · 42 min · recovery*

**Solo**
```
Active recovery: 30 min easy + 12 min mobility

Part A:
- 30 min easy row (zone 1-2, nose breathing)

Part B (2-3 slow rounds, 12 min):
- cat-cow: 45 s
- world's greatest stretch: 45 s
- calf raises off a step (slow): 45 s
- ankle dorsiflexion rocks: 45 s
- side plank: 45 s
- one-sided moves: 45 s per side
```
**Team of 2**
```
Team of 2: Active recovery: 30 min easy + 12 min mobility
Do it together: easy chat pace for part A, then take turns holding each stretch while your partner keeps time.

Part A:
- 30 min easy row (zone 1-2, nose breathing)

Part B (2-3 slow rounds, 12 min):
- cat-cow: 45 s
- world's greatest stretch: 45 s
- calf raises off a step (slow): 45 s
- ankle dorsiflexion rocks: 45 s
- side plank: 45 s
- one-sided moves: 45 s per side
```
Score: None


## Week 2

### Day 8 · Monday · Long Current
*Strength endurance · 44 min · moderate*

**Solo**
```
Part A: every 3 min x 5 | Part B: AMRAP 26 min

Part A (15 min) (Every 3 min x 5 sets, heavy but unbroken):
- 20 m DB farmers lunges (2x22.5/15 kg)
- 25 DB push press (2x15/10 kg)
Rest 3 min

Part B (26 min) (AMRAP 26 min):
- 600 m run
- 25 wall balls (6/4 kg)
- 12 burpee broad jumps
- 30 V-ups
```
**Team of 2**
```
Team of 2: Part A: every 3 min x 5 | Part B: AMRAP 26 min
Same clock for both partners; how to share each part is shown on the part.

Part A (15 min) (Every 3 min x 5 sets, heavy but unbroken):
You go, I go: P1 goes at the top of each interval, P2 starts the moment P1 finishes. Both do the full work.
- 20 m DB farmers lunges (2x22.5/15 kg)
- 25 DB push press (2x15/10 kg)
Rest 3 min

Part B (26 min) (AMRAP 26 min):
You go, I go: P1 does a full round, then P2. Bigger rounds because you rest while your partner works.
- 600 m run
- 40 wall balls (6/4 kg)
- 18 burpee broad jumps
- 45 V-ups
```
Score: Part A load used; Part B rounds + reps
